*SETPUR
820-A1
This X12 Transaction Set contains the format and establishes the data 
contents of the Payment Order/Remittance Advice Transaction Set (820) for 
use within the context of an Electronic Data Interchange (EDI) environment. 
The transaction set can be used to make a payment, send a remittance advice, 
or make a payment and send a remittance advice.This transaction set can be an order to a financial institution to make a 
payment to a payee. It can also be a remittance advice identifying the 
detail needed to perform cash application to the payee's accounts receivable 
system. The remittance advice can go directly from payer to payee, through a 
financial institution, or through a third party agent.
*SEGPUR
ADX
To convey accounts-payable adjustment information for the purpose of cash 
application, including payer-generated debit/credit memos
*SEGPUR
BPR
To indicate the beginning of a Payment Order/Remittance Advice Transaction 
Set and total payment amount, or to enable related transfer of funds and/or 
information from payer to payee to occur
*SEGPUR
CUR
To specify the currency (dollars, pounds, francs, etc.) used in a 
transaction
*SEGPUR
DTM
To specify pertinent dates and times
*SEGPUR
ENT
To designate the entities which are parties to a transaction and specify a 
reference meaningful to those entities
*SEGPUR
IT1
To specify the basic and most frequently used line item data for the invoice 
and related transactions
*SEGPUR
N1
To identify a party by type of organization, name, and code
*SEGPUR
N2
To specify additional names
*SEGPUR
N3
To specify the location of the named party
*SEGPUR
N4
To specify the geographic place of the named party
*SEGPUR
NM1
To supply the full name of an individual or organizational entity
*SEGPUR
NTE
To transmit information in a free-form format, if necessary, for comment or 
special instruction
*SEGPUR
PER
To identify a person or office to whom administrative communications should 
be directed
*SEGPUR
RDM
To identify remittance delivery when remittance is separate from payment
*SEGPUR
REF
To specify identifying information
*SEGPUR
RMR
To specify the accounts receivable open item(s) to be included in the cash 
application and to convey the appropriate detail
*SEGPUR
SAC
To request or identify a service, promotion, allowance, or charge; to 
specify the amount or percentage for the service, promotion, allowance, or 
charge
*SEGPUR
SE
To indicate the end of the transaction set and provide the count of the 
transmitted segments (including the beginning (ST) and ending (SE) segments)
*SEGPUR
SLN
To specify product subline detail item data
*SEGPUR
ST
To indicate the start of a transaction set and to assign a control number
*SEGPUR
TRN
To uniquely identify a transaction to an application
*SEGNTE
ADX,03,N,1
P0304
*SEGNTE
ADX,01,S,1
ADX01 specifies the amount of the adjustment and must be signed if negative. 
If negative, it reduces the payment amount; if positive, it increases the 
payment amount.
*SEGNTE
ADX,02,S,1
ADX02 specifies the reason for claiming the adjustment.
*SEGNTE
ADX,03,S,1
ADX03 and ADX04 specify the identification of the adjustment.
*SEGNTE

*SEGNTE
BPR,02,S,1
BPR02 specifies the payment amount.
*SEGNTE
BPR,06,S,1
When using this transaction set to initiate a payment, all or some of BPR06 
through BPR16 may be required, depending on the conventions of the specific 
financial channel being used.
*SEGNTE
BPR,06,S,1
BPR06 and BPR07 relate to the originating depository financial institution 
(ODFI).
*SEGNTE
BPR,08,S,1
BPR08 is a code identifying the type of bank account or other financial 
asset.
*SEGNTE
BPR,09,S,1
BPR09 is the account of the company originating the payment. This account 
may be debited or credited depending on the type of payment order.
*SEGNTE
BPR,10,S,1
BPR10 shall be mutually established between the originating depository 
financial institution (ODFI) and the company originating the payment.
*SEGNTE
BPR,12,S,1
BPR12 and BPR13 relate to the receiving depository financial institution 
(RDFI).
*SEGNTE
BPR,14,S,1
BPR14 is a code identifying the type of bank account or other financial 
asset.
*SEGNTE
BPR,15,S,1
BPR15 is the account number of the receiving company to be debited or 
credited with the payment order.
*SEGNTE
BPR,16,S,1
BPR16 is the date the originating company intends for the transaction to be 
settled (i.e., Payment Effective Date).
*SEGNTE
BPR,17,S,1
BPR17 is a code identifying the business reason for this payment.
*SEGNTE
BPR,18,S,1
BPR18, BPR19, BPR20 and BPR21, if used, identify a third bank identification 
number and account to be used for return items only.
*SEGNTE
BPR,20,S,1
BPR20 is a code identifying the type of bank account or other financial 
asset.

ENT,08,N,1

*SEGNTE
ENT,00,C,1
This segment allows for the grouping of data by entity/entities at or within 
a master/masters. A master (e.g., an organization) can be comprised of 
numerous subgroups (e.g., entities). This master may send grouped data to 
another master (e.g., an organization) which is comprised of one or more 
entities. Groupings are as follows:
*SEGNTE
ENT,00,C,1
(1) Single/Single: Only ENT01 is necessary, because there is a single entity 
(the sending master) communicating with a single entity (the receiving 
master).
*SEGNTE
ENT,00,C,1
(2) Single/Multiple: ENT05, ENT06, and ENT07 would be used to identify the 
entities within the receiving master. The sending master is a single entity, 
so no other data elements need be used.
*SEGNTE
ENT,00,C,1
(3) Multiple/Single: ENT02, ENT03, and ENT04 would be used to identify the 
entities within the sending master. The receiving master is a single entity, 
so no other data elements need be used.
*SEGNTE
ENT,00,C,1
(4) Multiple/Multiple: ENT02, ENT03, and ENT04 would be used to identify the 
entities within the sending master. ENT05, ENT06, and ENT07 would be used to 
identify the entities within the receiving master.
*SEGNTE
ENT,00,C,1
This segment also allows for the transmission of a unique reference number 
that is meaningful between the entities.

N1,04,C,1
This segment, used alone, provides the most efficient method of providing 
organizational identification. To obtain this efficiency the "ID Code" 
(N104) must provide a key to the table maintained by the transaction 
processing party.
*SEGNTE

*SEGNTE
NTE,00,C,1
The NTE segment permits free-form information/data which, under ANSI X12 
standard implementations, is not machine processible. The use of the NTE 
segment should therefore be avoided, if at all possible, in an automated 
environment.

*SEGNTE
RDM,02,C,1
RDM02 is used to contain the name of a third party processor if needed, who 
would be the first recipient of the remittance.
*SEGNTE
RDM,03,C,1
RDM03 contains the operative communication number for the delivery method 
specified in RDM01 (i.e. fax phone number and mail address).
*SEGNTE
RDM,04,C,1
RDM04 and RDM05 allow for additional room to convey further routing 
information beyond what is given in RDM03.
*SEGNTE
REF,02,N,1
R0203
*SEGNTE
REF,04,S,1
REF04 contains data relating to the value cited in REF02.
*SEGNTE
RMR,01,N,1
P0102
*SEGNTE
RMR,07,N,1
P0708
*SEGNTE
RMR,03,S,1
If RMR03 is present, it specifies how the cash is to be applied.
*SEGNTE
RMR,04,S,1
RMR04 is the amount paid.
*SEGNTE
RMR,05,S,1
RMR05 is the amount of invoice (including charges, less allowance) before 
terms discount (if discount is applicable) or debit amount or credit amount 
of referenced items.
*SEGNTE
RMR,06,S,1
RMR06 is the amount of discount taken.
*SEGNTE
RMR,08,S,1
RMR08, if present, represents an interest penalty payment, amount late 
interest paid, or amount anticipation.
*SEGNTE
RMR,01,C,1
Parties using this segment should agree on the content of RMR01 and RMR02 
prior to initiating communication.
*SEGNTE
RMR,03,C,1
If RMR03 is not present, this is a payment for an open item. If paying an 
open item, RMR02 must be present. If not paying a specific open item, RMR04 
must be present.
*SEGNTE
RMR,05,C,1
RMR05 may be needed by some payees to distinguish between duplicate 
reference numbers.
*SEGNTE
SAC,02,N,1
R0203
*SEGNTE
SAC,03,N,1
P0304
*SEGNTE
SAC,06,N,1
P0607
*SEGNTE
SAC,09,N,1
P0910

SAC,11,N,1

SAC,14,N,1

*SEGNTE
SAC,16,N,1
C1615
*SEGNTE
SAC,01,S,1
If SAC01 is "A" or "C", then at least one of SAC05, SAC07, or SAC08 is 
required.
*SEGNTE
SAC,05,S,1
SAC05 is the total amount for the service, promotion, allowance, or charge.
*SEGNTE
SAC,05,S,1
If SAC05 is present with SAC07 or SAC08, then SAC05 takes precedence.
*SEGNTE
SAC,08,S,1
SAC08 is the allowance or charge rate per unit.
*SEGNTE
SAC,10,S,1
SAC10 and SAC11 is the quantity basis when the allowance or charge quantity 
is different from the purchase order or invoice quantity.
*SEGNTE
SAC,10,S,1
SAC10 and SAC11 used together indicate a quantity range, which could be a 
dollar amount, that is applicable to service, promotion, allowance, or 
charge.
*SEGNTE
SAC,13,S,1
SAC13 is used in conjunction with SAC02 or SAC04 to provide a specific 
reference number as identified by the code used.
*SEGNTE
SAC,14,S,1
SAC14 is used in conjunction with SAC13 to identify an option when there is 
more than one option of the promotion.
*SEGNTE
SAC,16,S,1
SAC16 is used to identify the language being used in SAC15.
*SEGNTE
SAC,04,C,1
SAC04 may be used to uniquely identify the service, promotion, allowance, or 
charge. In addition, it may be used in conjunction with SAC03 to further 
define SAC02.
*SEGNTE
SAC,10,C,1
In some business applications, it is necessary to advise the trading partner 
of the actual dollar amount that a particular allowance, charge, or 
promotion was based on to reduce ambiguity. This amount is commonly referred 
to as "Dollar Basis Amount". It is represented in the SAC segment in SAC10 
using the qualifier "DO" - Dollars in SAC09.
*SEGNTE
SE,00,C,1
SE is the last segment of each transaction set.

*SEGNTE
SLN,01,S,1
SLN01 is the identifying number for the subline item.
*SEGNTE
SLN,02,S,1
SLN02 is the identifying number for the subline level. The subline level is 
analogous to the level code used in a bill of materials.
*SEGNTE
SLN,03,S,1
SLN03 is the configuration code indicating the relationship of the subline 
item to the baseline item.
*SEGNTE
SLN,08,S,1
SLN08 is a code indicating the relationship of the price or amount to the 
associated segment.
*SEGNTE
SLN,00,C,1
See the Data Element Dictionary for a complete list of IDs.
*SEGNTE
SLN,01,C,1
SLN01 is related to (but not necessarily equivalent to) the baseline item 
number. Example: 1.1 or 1A might be used as a subline number to relate to 
baseline number 1.
*SEGNTE
SLN,09,C,1
SLN09 through SLN28 provide for ten different product/service IDs for each 
item. For example: Case, Color, Drawing No., U.P.C. No., ISBN No., Model 
No., or SKU.
*SEGNTE
ST,01,S,1
The transaction set identifier (ST01) is used by the translation routines of 
the interchange partners to select the appropriate transaction set 
definition (e.g., 810 selects the Invoice Transaction Set).
*SEGNTE
ST,03,S,1
The implementation convention reference (ST03) is used by the translation 
routines of the interchange partners to select the appropriate 
implementation convention to match the transaction set definition. When 
used, this implementation convention reference takes precedence over the 
implementation reference specified in the GS08.
*SEGNTE
TRN,02,S,1
TRN02 provides unique identification for the transaction.
*SEGNTE
TRN,03,S,1
TRN03 identifies an organization.
*SEGNTE
TRN,04,S,1
TRN04 identifies a further subdivision within the organization.
